# WPF Gallery Milestone 1 Tracker

Last updated: 2026-05-26

## Goal

Make ModernWpf Gallery visually near-identical to the official WPF Gallery for
all WPF Gallery-equivalent pages and controls, while keeping ModernWpf and
WinUI-style controls such as `NavigationView` where they fit the WinUI Gallery
interaction model.

## Source References

| Source | Local path | Use |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Official WPF Gallery | `D:\repos\WPF-Samples\Sample Applications\WPFGallery` | Primary visual, page, copy, sample, and catalog reference. |
| Official WinUI Gallery | `D:\repos\WinUI-Gallery` | Primary source and visual reference for ModernWpf/WinUI extension pages and WinUI control pages implemented in WPF. |
| ModernWpf Gallery | `ModernWpf.Gallery` | Target implementation. |
| Gallery runtime tests | `test\ModernWpf.Gallery.Tests` | Main regression layer for route, page, shell, catalog, and sample parity checks. |

Use the local checkouts above as the source of truth. Do not web-search WinUI
Gallery source while `D:\repos\WinUI-Gallery` is available.

WinUI Gallery extension scope: include WinUI Gallery pages only when the page is
for a ModernWpf-implemented WinUI control or a control surface intentionally
implemented in WPF. Do not add standalone WinUI concept/API pages such as
`EasingFunction`, `PageTransition`, animation interop, connected animation,
implicit transitions, theme transitions, AppWindow/windowing, shell
notifications, WebView2, MapControl, MediaPlayerElement, Sound,
StoragePickers, StandardUICommand, or XamlUICommand just because they exist in
WinUI Gallery.

Deleted page source ownership: when a page is intentionally removed from the
gallery, remove its active source too. That includes catalog metadata,
navigation routes, sample factory cases, sample-code files, curated automation
rows, visual-check mappings, and documentation that describes the page as
currently implemented. Keep only incidental API usage that belongs to a retained
page, historical tracker notes, or generic WPF/framework type names used by a
retained page. Do not keep hard-coded negative page lists as the primary guard;
prefer positive catalog/resource assertions for the retained surface.

Current ModernWpf/WinUI extension surface: keep only extension pages backed by
actual ModernWpf controls or explicitly retained WPF implementations:
`NavigationView`, `InfoBar`, `NumberBox`, `AutoSuggestBox`, `ContentDialog`,
`TeachingTip`, `CommandBar`, `CommandBarFlyout`, `AppBarButton`,
`AppBarToggleButton`, `AppBarSeparator`, `DropDownButton`, `SplitButton`,
`ToggleSplitButton`, `RepeatButton`, `ToggleButton`, `MenuBar`, `MenuFlyout`,
`ItemsRepeater`, `PipsPager`, `RatingControl`, `ToggleSwitch`, `ColorPicker`,
`HyperlinkButton`, `ProgressRing`, `InfoBadge`, `Flyout`, `Popup`, `Pivot`,
`BreadcrumbBar`, `SelectorBar`, `SplitView`, `AnnotatedScrollBar`,
`ParallaxView`, `PullToRefresh`, `GridView`, `SwipeControl`, `PersonPicture`,
`IconElement`, `ThemeShadow`, and `TitleBar`.
Do not keep WinUI alias pages for `CalendarDatePicker`, `CalendarView`,
`TimePicker`, `TabView`, `RichEditBox`, `RichTextBlock`, `ScrollViewer`,
`ScrollView`, `FlipView`, or `ItemsView`; retained WPF pages and retained
ModernWpf pages may still use those words incidentally in examples or framework
types. Older tracker notes for pruned pages are historical evidence only; do
not treat them as current implementation status or as work to preserve.

## Copy vs Adapt Rule

Prefer copying official WPF Gallery page structure, sample XAML, page titles,
descriptions, images, spacing, and labels when the target page is a direct WPF
Gallery equivalent.

Adapt instead of raw-copying when the source depends on:

- `WPFGallery` namespaces, navigation services, or view-model plumbing that does
  not exist in ModernWpf Gallery.
- Official WPF Gallery resource keys, converters, or helpers that need to map
  to ModernWpf resource names or local helper controls.
- ModernWpf-specific routing, catalog aliases, test hooks, or automation IDs.
- Pages where ModernWpf intentionally keeps a distinct WinUI/ModernWpf control
  page next to WPF Gallery pages because the control is actually implemented in
  ModernWpf, such as retained `TitleBar`, `ThemeShadow`, `Pivot`, or
  `NavigationView` surfaces. Do not keep WinUI aliases for WPF-only pages such
  as `CalendarView` or `RichEditBox`.
- Controls or interactions that WPF Gallery implements with app-local helpers
  but ModernWpf already has an equivalent reusable control.

In short: the official WPF Gallery is the source of truth for WPF-equivalent
visuals, but most files still need namespace, resource, route, and test
adaptation before they can live in this repo.

## Architecture Direction

The current broad parity pass got WPF Gallery-equivalent pages represented, but
it still relies too much on ModernWpf-specific sample factories that recreate
official WPF Gallery pages in C#. That makes visual parity slower and easier to
drift.

The preferred next architecture is closer to WPF Gallery for WPF-equivalent
pages:

- Keep the ModernWpf outer shell, catalog, and `NavigationView` model so the
  gallery can still host ModernWpf/WinUI-specific pages.
- Add or strengthen a WPF Gallery compatibility layer inside that shell:
  WPF-style page view models with `PageTitle`, `PageDescription`, and sample
  code strings; WPF Gallery-like `PageHeader`, `ControlExample`, `HeaderTile`,
  and navigation-card components; and explicit catalog item to page mappings.
- Prefer adapted `.xaml` pages copied from official WPF Gallery for direct
  WPF-equivalent pages instead of rebuilding those pages through C# sample
  factories.
- Keep C# sample factories for ModernWpf/WinUI-specific pages, generated sample
  content, or pages that do not exist in official WPF Gallery.
- Treat the official WPF Gallery view model/resource/navigation shape as the
  default design for WPF pages, then adapt only the integration points required
  by ModernWpf routing, resource naming, tests, and mixed WPF/WinUI catalog
  support.

This should make future parity rounds more mechanical: copy official page XAML
and view-model state, adapt namespaces/resources/routes, add targeted tests, and
record the checklist status below.

Current architecture status: top-level WPF Gallery-equivalent pages now keep
their default ModernWpf routing constructors while also exposing official-style
constructor-injected view-model paths for Home, All Controls, What's New,
Settings, and WPF-equivalent section pages. The retained ModernWpf shell now
routes WPF Gallery section groups through official-named section page subclasses
while keeping generic `SectionPage` only for ModernWpf extension groups. Home,
All Controls, What's New, and section page constructors now follow the official
WPF Gallery `Views` order of `InitializeComponent()`, then `ViewModel`, then
`DataContext`; Settings keeps its matching official Settings order. The copied
WPF item pages that use this same official init-first constructor shape now
match it as well: Colors, Icons, Typography, Spacing, Geometry, and User
Dashboard. This keeps shell navigation unchanged while moving page construction
closer to the official WPF Gallery `Views` pattern.
Copied/adapted WPF Gallery page and support control code-behind declarations
now also keep the official unsealed class shape (`public partial class`) for
Home, All Controls, What's New, Settings, direct item/support pages, section
page adapters, `HeaderTile`, and `TileGallery`, while local shell-only classes
such as `ItemPage` remain outside that source-shape rule.
Copied item page code-behind now also keeps the official member order for the
page-specific `ViewModel` property before the copied constructor where the WPF
Gallery source uses that shape: Basic Input, Collections, Date & Time, selected
Design Guidance pages, Media, selected Navigation pages, User Dashboard, Status
& Info, System, and selected Text pages. Verified official constructor-first
exceptions such as Layout pages, Hyperlink, Frame, NavigationWindow,
Iconography, and Typography remain constructor-first.
Navigation item pages now preserve the official copied root namespace/content
declaration and PageHeader/ScrollViewer source shape, with Frame and
NavigationWindow also keeping the official compact page-root declaration order.
Menu also preserves the official copied glyph menu item header `TextBlock`
declarations, menu-item style/event-setter, and click-handler source shape.
Frame and NavigationWindow support pages now also preserve the official
copied window-launcher XAML/source shape, and the copied `FrameWindow`,
`Page1`, and `Page2` support XAML root namespace/content declaration source
shape while retaining ModernWpf route adaptations.
TabControl now preserves the official copied tab-header placeholder and
constructor spacing source shape.
Text item pages now preserve the official copied root namespace/content
declaration and PageHeader/ScrollViewer source shape, with TextBox also
keeping the official helper namespace order, Hyperlink keeping the official
compact page-root declaration order and nested header/scroll paragraph shape,
and Label/TextBox keeping representative official input-sample source details.
TextBlock, RichTextEdit, PasswordBox, and Hyperlink also keep representative
official sample declaration order, with
TextBlock retaining the local explicit whitespace entities needed to avoid
source trailing spaces. The copied Text pages also keep official final
StackPanel/ScrollViewer/Grid closing paragraphs, and Hyperlink keeps the
official nested Hyperlink indentation and final outer-Grid paragraph.
Status & Info item pages now preserve the official copied root
namespace/content declaration and PageHeader/ScrollViewer source shape, with
ProgressBar also keeping representative official determinate/indeterminate
sample declaration order and ToolTip also keeping the official sample button
attribute order. ProgressBar and ToolTip also keep the official simple
constructor paragraph before `InitializeComponent()`.
System item pages now preserve the official copied root/content declaration and
PageHeader/ScrollViewer source shape and the official `ControlExample`
`HeaderText` / `XamlCode` / `CSharpCode` attribute order. File and Folder
Dialogs, MessageBox, and Clipboard now also keep representative official live
sample control declaration order while retaining local target-framework and
Clipboard image asset adaptations. File and Folder Dialogs and MessageBox also
keep the official final `ControlExample` to `StackPanel` closing paragraph, and
MessageBox also keeps the official live `MessageBox.Show(...)` call/comment
source shape while retaining local button/image helper adaptations.
Date & Time and Media item pages now preserve the official copied root
namespace/content declaration and PageHeader/ScrollViewer source shape, with
Calendar also keeping the official compact sample `Calendar` declaration,
DatePicker keeping the official simple sample declaration order, Canvas keeping
the official Viewbox/Canvas/Path declaration order, and Media preserving the
official PageHeader title/description attribute order. Calendar, DatePicker,
Canvas, and Image also keep the official simple constructor paragraph before
`InitializeComponent()`.
Basic Input item pages now preserve the official copied root namespace/content
declaration and PageHeader/ScrollViewer source shape, with Button also keeping
the official commented icon-sample placeholders, CheckBox keeping the official
compact first sample declaration, and RadioButton keeping representative
official sample container and attribute order, ComboBox keeping representative
official inline/items-source/editable sample declaration order, and Slider
keeping representative official output-grid/value-binding declaration order plus
the official StackPanel/ScrollViewer closing paragraph.
Collections item pages now preserve the official copied root namespace/model
declarations and PageHeader/ScrollViewer source shape, with ListBox/ListView
also keeping the official commented sample source placeholders, DataGrid and
TreeView keeping representative official live sample declaration order, DataGrid
keeping the official single-sample closing paragraph, and ListView keeping
representative official selection-mode label and GridViewColumn declaration
order.
Layout item pages now preserve the official copied root namespace/content
declaration and PageHeader/ScrollViewer source shape, with GridSplitter also
keeping the official `xmlns:sys` declaration, official compact page-root
declaration order, and current official multiline sample-code source shape,
Expander also keeping the official copied ExpandDirection TODO marker,
Grid/GroupBox/ResizeGrip also keeping the official compact page-root
declaration order, and ResizeGrip keeping the official sample-code/button
attribute order. Border, Grid, GridSplitter, GroupBox,
ResizeGrip, and StackPanel now also keep representative official live sample
declaration order while preserving local WPF/accessibility adaptations such as
Grid's expanded live row/column definitions for the official shorthand sample,
GridSplitter's explicit resize directions, and GroupBox's TextBox automation
names; GroupBox also keeps the current official live Submit button declaration
while preserving the official sample-code width text.
Design Guidance item pages now preserve the official copied root namespace/content
declaration and PageHeader/scroll-host source shape where the official pages use
it, with Color keeping the official compact page-root declaration order, named
`ContentPagePane` paragraph, and selector/frame declaration order,
Typography/Spacing/Geometry preserving the official compact page-root
declaration order and unnamed root shape, Geometry keeping the official fixed
image-host declaration order, and Iconography keeping the official compact
no-local page-root declaration order plus representative instructions/search,
details-pane, and pagination declaration order while retaining the local
Loaded-handler, behavior-trigger, and asset adaptations.
Color's six subsection pages now also preserve the official compact root
declaration order, including the official subsection `xmlns:local` declaration
and Background/Signal foreground-before-title order, while the Text subsection
also preserves representative official copied comment, `ColorPageExample`,
panel, and Text-on-Accent tile declaration order.
Samples/User Dashboard now also preserves representative official copied XAML
declaration source shape for the page root, resource style, user-list host,
list item style, `NewUserButton` adjacency, user-detail header/avatar stack,
form input controls, command-status tail, and command buttons while retaining
local model namespace, heading-level adapter, and target-framework notification
guard adaptations. Its copied code-behind also preserves representative
official resize-handler branch/spacing shape and the age-notification local
declaration while keeping the local target-framework notification wrapper.
Settings now also preserves representative official copied XAML declaration
source shape for its resource setters, header and scroll host, appearance card,
theme selector, About card, dependency links, and warranty group while retaining
the accepted ModernWpf root style, local asset URI, entity-escaped text, and
forced visual-test theme adaptations.
All Controls now also preserves representative official copied AllSamples
declaration source shape for its root rows, PageHeader, ScrollViewer, and
navigation-card ItemsControl order while retaining ModernWpf root style and
design-time attribute adaptations.
The shared section page now also preserves representative official copied
section declaration source shape for root rows, PageHeader, and the direct
navigation-card ItemsControl order while retaining ModernWpf root style,
design-time attribute, and extension-section scroll-host adaptations.
Home now also preserves representative official copied Dashboard declaration
source shape for the retained content-root scroll/grid rows, hero/title block,
TileGallery, and overview/recently-added navigation-card ItemsControl order
while retaining the local diagnostic root wrapper, design-time attribute,
pack-URI, and heading adapter adaptations.
What's New now also preserves representative official copied declaration source
shape for its page resources, `ContentPagePane` root, PageHeader, and ScrollViewer
order while retaining the accepted ModernWpf root style adapter.
Retained shell High Contrast source shape now also pins the official WPF Gallery
title-bar/default-close/footer hover trigger pattern, the `HighContrastBorder`
thickness literal, the Back/title source order, `MainWindow` high-contrast border
and non-client-frame update path, and the retained `NavigationView` alias refresh
on `SystemParameters.HighContrast` while keeping the accepted NavigationView
shell adaptation.
ModernWpf `ThemeResourceExtension` `SystemColor*` bindings now also refresh on
both entering and exiting `SystemParameters.HighContrast`, and on any
system-parameter change while High Contrast is active, so WPF Fluent
HighContrast resource colors are not left stale after broader OS
High Contrast palette transitions. The theme test coverage also now pins that
every `SystemColor*Color` key referenced by `HighContrast.xaml` is backed by a
`ThemeResourceExtension` system-color source property.
The generic `ItemPage` wrapper now also avoids normal-runtime local-only
`PageHeader` and `DirectPageContentHost` names plus `GallerySampleHost`
automation IDs while keeping source-shaped `ControlExample` binding order and
the retained `GallerySample_*` sample-level automation IDs used by diagnostics.

## Commit Policy

Commit after each coherent milestone round, but avoid small commits. Fold tiny
documentation-only notes, isolated cleanup, and minor tracker wording tweaks into
the next substantive alignment or verification commit unless the user explicitly
asks for a standalone commit.

## Done

| Area | Status | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Official WPF Gallery source checkout | Done | Cloned under `D:\repos\WPF-Samples`. |
| WPF Gallery catalog sections | Done | WPF Gallery-equivalent sections are represented, including Design Guidance, Samples, Basic Input, Collections, Date & Calendar, Layout, Media, Navigation, Status & Info, Text, and System. The visible navigation order now keeps the official WPF section sequence contiguous through System, then places the orphaned Media section and the ModernWpf controls extension group after the official block. |
| ModernWpf/WinUI page preservation | Done | Distinct ModernWpf pages are kept where aliases overlap WPF pages. WinUI Gallery extension pages are kept only when they represent an implemented ModernWpf control/control surface; non-control Motion pages and non-ModernWpf platform/API pages have been omitted from catalog search/navigation, curated automation defaults, and the default visual-check list. |
| Page shell and header model | Mostly done | Generic non-direct `ItemPage` wrapper headers, direct-page wrapper header collapse, section, support, sample chrome, Home header tile strip with Light/Dark acrylic and HighContrast button-fill coverage, Home hero/section heading declarations, navigation-card resources, category/all-controls headers, What's New, Settings, WPF Gallery-style navigation page binding paths, WPF Gallery-style top-level `Page` roots for Home/section/All Controls/What's New/Settings, official-named WPF section page subclasses, official WPF section `Page.Title` values, hidden shell `Frame` content hosting, WPF Gallery-style 44px app title row, WPF Gallery-style `ViewModel.ApplicationTitle` binding for both the window title and visible title text, WPF Gallery-style Back button `ViewModel.BackCommand` / `ViewModel.CanNavigateback` bindings, WPF Gallery-style Settings footer command routed through `MainWindowViewModel.SettingsCommand` with the existing `BindingProxy` bridging detached retained `NavigationView.PaneFooter` content, official `WindowBackground` shell background resource, official code-created `WindowChrome` setup, official high-contrast/Windows 11 `WindowChrome.NonClientFrameEdges` updates, source-shape coverage for the shell `HighContrastBorder`, title-bar/default-close/footer high-contrast hover triggers, Back/title source order, and retained `NavigationView` high-contrast alias refresh, hidden NavigationView pane toggle/header/search chrome, official `Navigation Pane` automation naming, NavigationView item glyph/text columns, full-state retained NavigationView left-pane resource aliases to WPF Gallery TreeView tokens with Light/Dark refresh and explicit HighContrast system-brush reference coverage, WPF Gallery-style top-level and child row insets, WPF Gallery-style left disclosure chevrons for section groups, tight title text UIA bounds, 42px row rhythm aligned to the official TreeView, startup-collapsed shell navigation groups, adapted official `SettingsButton` footer/separator geometry and automation notification, neutralized retained `NavigationView` content-grid chrome, adapted content-frame geometry, normal-mode suppression of ModernWpf-only visual-test status hooks, shell-owned named content diagnostic/artifact roots (`ContentRootGrid` for retained shell roots, official `ContentPagePane` where the reference page uses it, and direct-page wrapper `GalleryItemPageRoot` artifacts for copied pages whose official roots are unnamed) without non-official top-level root automation IDs, unnamed official section and All Controls roots/card lists located structurally, generic `ItemPage` wrapper artifact names now derived by diagnostics instead of root automation IDs, shared compatibility-control source shape without a non-official title `TextBlock` automation ID, and the official-style `Resources/PageStyles.xaml` / `Resources/Templates.xaml` resource layer have been moved toward WPF Gallery structure and spacing. The shared `BaseTextBlockStyle` now inherits foreground like official WPF Gallery instead of forcing `TextFillColorPrimaryBrush` from the style. |
| WPF Gallery typography and page style resources | Mostly done | WPF Gallery-equivalent typography sizes and shared page/card/color-panel styles now live in `ModernWpf.Gallery/Resources/PageStyles.xaml`, matching the official WPF Gallery resource-file shape while preserving ModernWpf-specific brushes where needed. `GalleryPageRootStyle` now inherits `BodyTextBlockFontSize` through `TextElement.FontSize` so top-level copied/support pages can use official-style unstyled body TextBlocks; `ColorTilesPanelStyle` uses the official `ControlExampleDisplayBrush` key like the official WPF Gallery source while intentionally leaving that missing key unresolved so color tile panels stay transparent over the page layer; the WPF Gallery resource layer overrides `SystemFillColorAttentionBrush` to `#0078D4` and `SurfaceStrokeColorDefaultBrush` to `#66757575` so copied Color tiles use the same WPF Fluent values as official WPF Gallery; and the 20 official User Dashboard `p*` image brush keys are exposed from the page-style dictionary with local assembly-qualified asset URIs. Runtime coverage verifies the merged dictionary, typography size keys, WPF Fluent brush overrides, root body font inheritance, base text style metrics, title/display style font sizes, missing-resource behavior, dynamic brush setter keys, User Dashboard image brush resources, and color tile panel geometry. |
| WPF Gallery navigation template resources | Mostly done | Shared `WrapPanelTemplate` and `NavigationCardTemplate` now live in `ModernWpf.Gallery/Resources/Templates.xaml`, matching the official WPF Gallery resource-file shape. Home, Section, and All Controls consume the app-level templates instead of per-page `GalleryCard.xaml` dictionaries; the card body text binding now uses the official WPF Gallery `Description` property name, the command binding now targets a WPF `Page` ancestor like official WPF Gallery, and `CommandParameter` now binds through the official `PageType` path with local `GalleryGroup`/`GalleryItem` shims that return the existing ModernWpf routing object. Runtime coverage verifies the merged dictionary, template geometry, navigation command binding path, card command-parameter path, card automation-name binding, card description binding path, `HeaderTile` button automation-name binding, and heading-level behavior. |
| WPF Gallery navigation view-model shape | Mostly done | The shell MainWindow now exposes an official-shaped `MainWindowViewModel` with `ApplicationTitle`, `BackCommand`, `SettingsCommand`, and `CanNavigateback` for title, Back button, and Settings footer command bindings. Home, What's New, section, All Controls, and Settings navigation-card/support pages also expose official-named or official-shaped WPF Gallery-style `ViewModel` objects: `DashboardPageViewModel`, `WhatsNewPageViewModel`, `AllSamplesPageViewModel`, `SettingsPageViewModel`, and section-specific `DesignGuidancePageViewModel`, `SamplesPageViewModel`, `BasicInputPageViewModel`, `CollectionsPageViewModel`, `DateAndTimePageViewModel`, `LayoutPageViewModel`, `MediaPageViewModel`, `NavigationPageViewModel`, `StatusAndInfoPageViewModel`, `TextPageViewModel`, and `SystemPageViewModel`. Section group routes now instantiate official-named section pages through `WpfGallerySectionPageFactory`, while the oversized ModernWpf extension group continues to use generic `SectionPage` with a scoped scroll host. Those VMs provide `NavigationCards`, `RecentlyAddedOrUpdatedSamplesInfo`, `PageTitle`, `PageDescription`, sample code strings, and `NavigateCommand` as appropriate; card models now expose official-style `Description` text while preserving generated-page header text on `PageDescription`. Page code-behind only adapts commands to ModernWpf routing callbacks, and the NavigationView shell now starts with section groups collapsed, uses WPF Gallery-like glyph/text item content columns, hides retained right-edge expand glyphs in favor of WPF Gallery-style left disclosure chevrons that rotate with group expansion, uses official `All Controls` caption casing, keeps the official WPF rows contiguous through System before extension groups, keeps title text UIA bounds tight to visible text, hides the built-in Settings item in favor of an adapted official footer `SettingsButton` whose command now navigates through `MainWindowViewModel.SettingsCommand` while its click handler is limited to the official automation notification, verifies that command-bound Settings path in the standalone shell parity test, and expands the selected group path during navigation from the WPF Gallery-style top Back button. |
| WPF Gallery direct page registry | Mostly done | WPF-equivalent direct pages now use explicit `WpfGalleryPageRegistry` item-to-page mappings inside the mixed ModernWpf shell. `ItemPage` consumes that registry, no longer falls back to generated C# recreations for direct WPF pages, keeps the wrapper `PageHeader` collapsed for registry-backed pages so copied direct content owns the official header, and now hosts all direct pages full-frame in a chrome-hidden `Frame` with `DirectPageContentMargin=0` so copied WPF `Page` roots have a valid parent and own their own scroll-root margins. Registry-created direct pages also receive the official inherited `BodyTextBlockFontSize`, closing the hidden-Frame text-inheritance gap for unstyled copied TextBlocks. Basic Input, Collections, Date & Calendar, Design Guidance, Layout, Media, Navigation, Samples, Status & Info, System, and Text item factories now construct their item pages with page-specific view models and WPF `Page` roots like official WPF Gallery. Runtime coverage verifies the registry's WPF-equivalent page set, instantiates every registered page, confirms registry-backed catalog items use direct hosting with empty generated example collections, zero direct host margin, hidden `Frame` navigation chrome, inherited body text size, and pins the converted Basic Input, Collections, Date & Calendar, Design Guidance, Layout, Media, Navigation, Samples, Status & Info, System, and Text direct roots. |
| Generated WPF page recreation factory | Done | The obsolete `WpfGalleryExampleFactory` C# recreation path has been removed. Direct WPF-equivalent pages are now copied/adapted XAML pages reached through the registry, and Color subpage selection uses a narrow `WpfGalleryColorSectionFactory` that returns adapted official `Page` sections for WPF Gallery-style frame navigation. |
| WPF Gallery compatibility `PageHeader` | Done | ModernWpf Gallery has an adapted WPF Gallery-style `PageHeader` control for copied WPF-equivalent pages; the shared template now uses the official `NullToVisibilityConverter` resource key, `TitleTextBlock` label name, title label automation name, heading level, tab stop, and tab index, with no local title `TextBlock` automation ID. It keeps the local heading-level adapter for multi-target/test support. |

## Historical WinUI Extension Notes (Superseded)

The notes below record earlier WinUI-extension implementation rounds. They are
historical evidence only. The retained ModernWpf/WinUI extension surface and
deleted-page source-ownership rule at the top of this tracker supersede any
older paragraph below that describes a pruned page, route, factory, sample-code
file, automation mapping, or visual-check target as currently implemented.
The generated ModernWpf NavigationView extension page now uses the local
official WinUI Gallery eight-example structure from
`D:\repos\WinUI-Gallery\WinUIGallery\Samples\ControlPages\NavigationViewPage.xaml`
/ `.xaml.cs`: default pane mode, top pane mode, adaptive pane orientation, tabs
with selection/focus behavior, data binding, footer menu items, hierarchical
menu items, and the API-in-action sample. `NavigationSampleFactory.CreateExamples`
now covers NavigationView as a source-backed Navigation WinUI example, consumes
the currently referenced local sample-code files without duplicate additional
sample-code expanders, keeps WinUI's `nvSample5`, `nvSample6`, `nvSample2`,
`nvSample7`, `nvSample4`, `nvSample9`, `nvSample8`, `nvSample`, and option
control names, and exposes `GallerySample_NavigationView_Root` /
`GallerySample_NavigationView_NavigationView` for runtime and visual checks.
The WPF adaptation uses real `ModernWpf.Controls.NavigationView` instances,
WPF `Frame` hosts, and the existing local tile sample content in place of
WinUI sample pages while preserving pane modes, footer items, hierarchy,
data-bound item templates, and option toggles. Current NavigationView
WinUI-reference evidence is
`artifacts/visual-checks/20260525-075704-742-52828/report.md` for Light and
`artifacts/visual-checks/20260525-075732-573-52396/report.md` for Dark, both
with ModernWpf and installed WinUI 3 Gallery `Passed`, nonblank app captures,
matching `745x460` primary crops, and primary deltas `6.41` / `5.77`. Avoid
reopening NavigationView's source shape unless a new WinUI source, runtime, or
crop regression appears. The live adaptive-orientation sample now mirrors the
local WinUI source `VisualStateManager` path by switching `nvSample2` to
`PaneDisplayMode=Top` once the rendered sample width crosses
`CompactModeThresholdWidth`; later rounds can separately investigate broader
NavigationView template/resource drift.

`ResetSampleBtn` names, and exposes `GallerySample_BreadcrumbBar_Root` /
`GallerySample_BreadcrumbBar_BreadcrumbBar` for runtime and visual checks. The
live WPF examples left-align the BreadcrumbBar controls so their measured crop
matches the installed WinUI Gallery's content-sized reference while the
displayed source snippet remains source-shaped.
Current BreadcrumbBar WinUI-reference evidence is
`artifacts/visual-checks/20260524-215636-420-67812/report.md` for Light and

with ModernWpf and installed WinUI 3 Gallery `Passed`; primary crop sizes are

harness includes BreadcrumbBar in the default WinUI extension control set and
uses a BreadcrumbBar-specific diagnostics artifact renderer from the real
control `ItemsSource` because the generic rendered `VisualBrush` artifact path
is blank for this repeater-based control; the renderer now follows the control
template's item and chevron padding so the final breadcrumb text is not clipped.
Avoid reopening BreadcrumbBar's source shape unless a new WinUI source,
runtime, or crop regression appears; a later round can separately investigate
remaining template-level glyph/antialiasing differences.
